### Module 21 tasks (PLAN-M21) · common rules
- Inputs for every task: `_meta/modules/M21.md` (its section, question or exercise rows, the budget, "Lab decision", "Facts established while planning" F1-F28, "Could not be read" C1-C15, the owner map), STYLE-GUIDE §3-§8, QUALITY-BAR B-E, SYLLABUS §4 (21), the module skeleton, the pilot `modules/17-signals.md` and the finished `modules/19-component-communication-and-projection.md` and `modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md` for Part D depth (read a section, answer or exercise of the same kind before writing; never copy), and `_meta/reviews/REVIEW-M20.md` (when it exists), `REVIEW-M19.md` and `REVIEW-M18.md` § Proposed FIX tasks. Re-verify every fact the task uses before writing (lab > package source > official docs/changelog) and record the basis next to the claim; every item under "Could not be read" for the task's section is read or probed first, or the claim is dropped. The facts of M21.md were probed 2026-10-09 on 22.2.1 (zone.js 0.16.3 in a scratch copy); a task re-runs the ones it quotes under `ng test`.
- **What runs and what is documented** (M21.md "Lab decision"): runtime claims are asserted by Angular specs under `ng test` (AOT test build, jsdom, zoneless TestBed); source reads name the file and function (`grep` first). **Zone-based specs need `zone.js`, which is not a lab dependency: it needs Jorge's decision before SEC-M21-03 (`npm install --save-dev zone.js@0.16.3` in `labs/angular`, edits package.json and the lockfile, done once and logged by the approving session). Without it, SEC-M21-03, EX-M21-03 and QB-M21-02 use the fallback of M21.md and write no zone claim as observed.** Otherwise the lab's `angular.json`, `package.json` and lockfiles are never edited, and a zone spec starts with `import 'zone.js';` (nothing else). **Documented, never worded as observed:** Angular DevTools Profiler UI, the Chrome performance track, production-mode behavior, reactive forms in zoneless apps, SSR stability, performance, the Angular team's rationale, Hibernate behavior. Scratch only under `mktemp -d` outside the repo, deleted at the end. Probes that need output under `ng test` collect it and fail an assertion to print it, in a scratch copy only. In a TestBed test, wrap the component under test in a host; observe template runs with a recorder array, assert "ran / did not run" sets, never counts (the dev check pass re-evaluates `Eager` views: F20). Only `labs/ts-js` files of EX-M21-06 touch `labs/ts-js`.
- **Corrections to apply from the plan (they override anything older):** no rationale or performance claim (C1). Status markers only in the STYLE-GUIDE §3.2 forms at the **first body mention in the module**, plain, with the versions of M21.md: OnPush default `[Changed in v22.0: …]`; `ChangeDetectionStrategy.Eager` `[Added in v21.2]` (`Default` deprecated per the typings, no version, C6); `provideZonelessChangeDetection` `[Stable since v20.2]`, default `[Changed in v21.0: …]`; `PendingTasks` `[Stable since v20.0]`, `PendingTasks.run` `[Developer preview since v19.0]`, `pendingUntilEvent` `[Developer preview since v20.0]`; `provideCheckNoChangesConfig` `[Developer preview since v20.0]`; `provideStabilityDebugging` `[Added in v21.1]`; `enableProfiling` `[Stable since v21.0]`; `ignoreChangesOutsideZone` `[Removed in v21.0]`; `useNgZoneOnStable` `[Removed in v20.0]`; `ChangeDetectorRef.checkNoChanges` `[Removed in v22.0]`. `NgZoneSchedulingMode` is not taught (C8). **State against the docs, as observations (C10):** TestBed "throws NG0100" held for an Eager root and not for an OnPush root; the "whole tree" wording of skipping-subtrees is zone-era. The CHANGELOG lists 22.2.2 while VERSIONS pins 22.2.1: write against 22.2.1 (C13). Q17.02's parenthetical "(with `Eager`, a tick would refresh the field)" does not match F16; QB tasks do not repeat it and tell the REVIEW. Every `ɵ`, `LView`, `TView`, flag and enum name is internal and **marked internal at its first use in each section**; flag and enum names come from the `v22.2.1` tag source, not the shipped chunks (F12).
- **Angular code** follows STYLE-GUIDE §7.1 (standalone, `inject()`, signals, signal inputs/outputs, built-in control flow, no `changeDetection` property unless the example is about it (the v22 default is OnPush; an `Eager` host is explicit and said so), v22 file names, `protected`/`readonly`, no `any`) and RUN.md §10 (at most 4 injected dependencies per class). **Legacy code** lives only under a `legacy/` folder or a `#### Legacy` heading, says what it is, and compiles under `ng build`; `ChangeDetectionStrategy.Default` and `checkNoChanges` appear only in labelled legacy or removed text. Fixtures use unique selectors and class names (`lab-m21-` prefix) and are standalone; no `console.log`.
- **First-time-pass rules (from REVIEW-M14 to M20):**
  - Factual slips: "which views refresh", "before versus after" and "first versus last" statements are checked against the recorded runs (F1-F21) before writing; every number in prose is traced to a test, run or read.
  - Mermaid: every section has a diagram of the type named in M21.md followed by a line starting `What to notice`; quote every label that has brackets, colons, `@`, `*`, `[` or parentheses; parse each diagram with the mermaid parser before finishing.
  - Misconceptions: **every** bullet gives the correction **and a cause clause**; "Once true: … until …" only from a source read in the task.
  - Terms: define at first use with an owner link (change detection, dirty checking, OnPush, zoneless, signal 17, `effect` 17, `ApplicationRef` 12 §6, `LView`/`TView` 12 §8, task and microtask 04 §1, `requestAnimationFrame` 08, flush Backend guide); run a first-use script over the module before finishing.
  - Code: each `### Code` subsection has numbered steps (QUALITY-BAR B5); sections 2, 3, 5, 6 and 8 have a `#### Legacy` subsection as M21.md says.
  - Mechanism: "How it actually works" answers at least one "why does it behave like that" from a source read, marked "read from the 22.2.1 source" with file and function named (each name found by `grep` in the named file), and "read, not run" where no test asserts it; never state a behavior from emitted code that a test does not assert.
  - Evidence: every "in the lab, X" names a test (`Section N: …`, `Q21.NN …`, `E21.N …`) asserting exactly X; every quoted runtime text keeps its `NGxxxx:` prefix; a test title never claims more than its assertions.
  - Titles: a question title counts what the answer gives; Output only where a spec asserts the answer exactly.
  - Links: questions linked from their section with their own `[Q21.NN](#q21-NN)`; answers link back; links to other modules carry a section or question anchor, not the bare file; `<!-- relink: … -->` only for a target not in the file yet; callouts begin `> [!NOTE]`/`> [!TIP]`/`> [!WARNING]`, the *Coming from the backend* callout states **Where the analogy breaks**.
  - Format: a blank line before every `###` heading and before a list that follows a bold label; no doubled commas; no "SYLLABUS" in reader text; exercise Constraints list what is not covered and only asserted behaviors are criteria.
  - SOURCES: every URL and package read gets a line in `SOURCES.md`, including code-source lines for each exercise and the outputs specs, the zone.js tarball, the `v22.2.1` docs and tag files, the CHANGELOG headings and the Hibernate 7.4 guide; a script lists URLs in the module not in SOURCES (must print nothing).
  - Repeats: tell each mechanism once and link it afterwards; the notification-to-flags path (§9), the walk modes (§4) and the scheduler (§5) are the three most likely to be retold in questions, and answers link the section.
- **Marker regions:** a section's region is its marker line plus the six empty `###` headings under it. A question chunk's region is its marker line plus the question anchors and headings up to the next marker (QB-M21-01 also writes the bank intro at its placeholder). An exercise's region is its marker line, its `<a id>` and its heading (EX-M21-01 also writes the exercise intro; SEC-M21-01 writes the verification paragraph at its placeholder). CLOSE-M21's marker is in `## Summary`; the placeholders for Short on time, Check your understanding and Connections name CLOSE-M21, and the header's Labs line carries a relink comment for CLOSE-M21.
- **Prose budget** (deltas over the module count measured just before the task; M21.md "Budget"): skeleton 1,726 measured 2026-10-09 by `node labs/tools/module-stats.mjs` and the QUALITY-BAR python one-liner (equal); SEC deltas 800 / 950 / 950 / 850 / 950 / 700 / 800 / 750 / 900 (7,650); EX 560 (intro included) + 5 × 520 (3,160); QB 7 × 1,130 (7,910; QB-M21-01 includes the bank intro of at most 40 words); CLOSE 800. **CLOSE at most 21,250 words; REVIEW ceiling 22,150.** Each task measures before and after with both counters, puts both counts in its LOG line, and cuts before finishing if more than 5% over its delta (repeats first; never a diagram, a trap's cause or a "because"). Answer parts average at most 225 words per QB chunk, none above 290. If SEC-M21-09 ends with the sections more than 3% over 7,650, it writes EX and QB caps here so the module still closes at 21,250.
- Do not repeat what another module owns (M21.md "Owned elsewhere"): the reactive graph and plain field versus signal 17; hook order, NG0100/NG0103 matrix, `refreshView` order 20; DevTools basics, compiler output 12; async pipe and impure pipes 14; projected content and `attachView` 19; forms 25; TestBed 29; performance workflow 31; SSR 32; AngularJS digest and migrations list 39; RxJS 22/23: one sentence plus a link.
- Module file: replace only the task's own `<!-- TASK: <ID> -->` region, at the end of the task, in a single edit. `<!-- PLACEHOLDER: … -->` lines are replaced by the task named in them. Never touch another task's marker. Labs: only the files M21.md "Lab files" assigns to the task, under `labs/angular/src/app/modules/21-change-detection/` (EX-M21-06: `labs/ts-js/src/modules/21-change-detection/`). No test needs the internet. Throwaway probes live only in a scratch directory and are deleted before the task ends.
- Every task adds its sources under `## Module 21 · Change detection` in `SOURCES.md` (after the last module section present; package reads as `<pkg>@<version>, <file>, read <date>`) and its inline-defined terms under `## Module 21` in `_meta/GLOSSARY-TERMS.md`.
- Acceptance for every task: the module's lab tests pass (`labs/angular`: `npx ng test --watch=false --include='src/app/modules/21-change-detection/**/*.spec.ts'` and `npx tsc -p tsconfig.spec.json --noEmit`, plus `npx ng build` when Angular code changed, which also compiles the `legacy/` fixtures); `node labs/tools/check-snippets.mjs . 21` and `node labs/tools/check-links.mjs --planned` exit 0; every URL in the module is in SOURCES.md; only the declared files are new or changed (`git status --short`); the task's marker is gone; no scratch or probe file is left behind; the LOG line gives the part's and the module's prose count.

### Module 22 tasks (PLAN-M22) · common rules
- Inputs for every task: `_meta/modules/M22.md` (its section, question or exercise rows, the budget, "Lab decision", "Facts established while planning" F1-F35, "Could not be read" C1-C13, the 22/23 split, the owner map), STYLE-GUIDE §3-§8, QUALITY-BAR B-E, SYLLABUS §4 (22), the module skeleton, the pilot `modules/17-signals.md` and the finished `modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md` and `modules/21-change-detection.md` for Part D depth (read a section, answer or exercise of the same kind before writing; never copy), and `_meta/reviews/REVIEW-M21.md` (when it exists), `REVIEW-M20.md` and `REVIEW-M19.md` § Proposed FIX tasks. Re-verify every fact the task uses before writing (lab > package source > official docs/changelog) and record the basis next to the claim; every item under "Could not be read" for the task's section is read or probed first, or the claim is dropped. The facts of M22.md were probed 2026-10-09 on `rxjs` 7.8.2 (Node 24.21.0; `ng test` in a scratch copy of `labs/angular`); a task re-runs the ones it quotes under `ng test`.
- **What runs and what is documented** (M22.md "Lab decision"): **`labs/ts-js` has no `rxjs` and nothing is installed: every runtime claim is asserted by Angular specs in `labs/angular` under `ng test`** (jsdom; `rxjs` 7.8.2 is already a dependency there; `vi.useFakeTimers` and `rxjs/testing` `TestScheduler.run` work). No task touches `labs/ts-js`, any `package.json`, `angular.json` or a lockfile. Source reads name the file and function (`grep` first, in `labs/angular/node_modules/rxjs/dist/esm/internal`). **Documented, never worded as observed:** the WICG `Observable` and browser support (C3), Project Reactor (C4), performance and memory (C7), RxJS 9 contents (C1), the TC39 proposal (C2), real-browser `fromEvent` beyond jsdom (C8), Angular application patterns (C10). Scratch only under `mktemp -d` outside the repo, deleted at the end. Probes that need output under `ng test` collect it and fail an assertion to print it, in a scratch copy only. Specs assert tables of values and frames, never wall-clock durations; `console.log` is not used (it is suppressed under `ng test`); `config.onUnhandledError` and fake timers are restored in `afterEach`; error classes are asserted by `name` (their constructors print as `…Impl`). `concepts/support/timeline.ts` is a non-spec file compiled by `ng build`: no `vi`, no `expect`.
- **Corrections to apply from the plan (they override anything older):** no rationale, roadmap or "what changes in v9" claim (C1). Status markers only in the STYLE-GUIDE §3.2 forms at the **first body mention in the module**, plain, with the versions of M22.md: `firstValueFrom`/`lastValueFrom` `[Added in v7.0]`; `toPromise` `[Deprecated since v7.0 — use firstValueFrom or lastValueFrom]`; separate `subscribe` callback arguments `[Deprecated since v6.4 — pass an observer]`; the multicast family `[Deprecated since v7.0 — use share, connectable or connect]`; instance operators `[Deprecated since v7.0 — use mergeWith]` (and `concatWith`, `zipWith`, `combineLatestWith`, `raceWith`); `pluck` `[Deprecated since v7.0 — use map and ?.]`; the `partition` operator `[Deprecated since v6.5 — use the partition function]`; `pairs` `[Deprecated since v7.0 — use from(Object.entries(obj))]`; top-level operator exports `[Added in v7.2]`; `mapTo` no version (C6); no marker on the `rxjs/operators` subpath (the docs call it deprecated, the typings do not: say both, C5). The typings' "will be removed in v8" refers to a release that never shipped (only `8.0.0-alpha.*` exist; `next` is `9.0.0-beta.1`): write "marked for removal in the next major" and cite the typings (F13, F33). **Name the 22/23 split**: higher-order mapping, `catchError`/`retry`/`finalize`, `share` configuration and `shareReplay`, schedulers, unsubscription strategies, `TestScheduler` authoring and the signals interop are module 23: one sentence plus a link, never a section. **`merge`, `concat`, `zip`, `combineLatest`, `race`, `partition` and `onErrorResumeNext` differ between `'rxjs'` and `'rxjs/operators'`** (F25): examples import from `'rxjs'`. Every `lift`, `operate`, `OperatorSubscriber`, `SafeSubscriber`, `ConsumerObserver`, `errorContext`, `timeoutProvider` and `_`-prefixed name is internal and **marked internal at its first use in each section**.
- **Angular code** follows STYLE-GUIDE §7.1 (standalone, `inject()`, signals, signal inputs/outputs, built-in control flow, v22 file names, `protected`/`readonly`, no `any`) and RUN.md §10 (at most 4 injected dependencies per class); most of this module is plain RxJS and needs no Angular class. **Legacy code** lives only under a `legacy/` folder or a `#### Legacy` heading, says what it is, and compiles under `ng build`. Fixtures use the `lab-m22-` prefix and are standalone; no `console.log`.
- **First-time-pass rules (from REVIEW-M14 to M21):**
  - Factual slips: "which value is emitted", "at which frame", "first versus last" and "before versus after complete" statements are checked against the recorded runs (F1-F35) before writing; every number in prose is traced to a test, run or read.
  - Mermaid: every section has a diagram of the type named in M22.md followed by a line starting `What to notice`; quote every label that has brackets, colons, `@`, `*`, `[` or parentheses; parse each diagram with the mermaid parser before finishing.
  - Misconceptions: **every** bullet gives the correction **and a cause clause**; "Once true: … until …" only from a source read in the task.
  - Terms: define at first use with an owner link (promise and microtask 04 §1 and §3, `AbortController` 04 §7, generics 07 §1, `async` pipe 14 §8, `toSignal` 17 §8, `EventEmitter` 19 §6, `takeUntilDestroyed` 20 §5, flush and `Flux`/`Mono` Backend guide); run a first-use script over the module before finishing.
  - Code: each `### Code` subsection has numbered steps (QUALITY-BAR B5); sections 1 to 7 have a `#### Legacy` subsection as M22.md says.
  - Mechanism: "How it actually works" answers at least one "why does it behave like that" from a source read, marked "read from the `rxjs` 7.8.2 source" with file and function named (each name found by `grep` in the named file), and "read, not run" where no test asserts it.
  - Evidence: every "in the lab, X" names a test (`Section N: …`, `Q22.NN …`, `E22.N …`) asserting exactly X; a test title never claims more than its assertions.
  - Titles: a question title counts what the answer gives; Output only where a spec asserts the answer exactly; Difference answers stay semantic and the paired Output answers print only the run.
  - Links: questions linked from their section with their own `[Q22.NN](#q22-NN)`; answers link back; links to other modules carry a section or question anchor, not the bare file; `<!-- relink: … -->` only for a target not in the file yet; callouts begin `> [!NOTE]`/`> [!TIP]`/`> [!WARNING]`, the *Coming from the backend* callout states **Where the analogy breaks**.
  - Format: a blank line before every `###` heading and before a list that follows a bold label; no doubled commas; no "SYLLABUS" in reader text; exercise Constraints list what is not covered and only asserted behaviors are criteria.
  - SOURCES: every URL and package read gets a line in `SOURCES.md`, including code-source lines for each exercise and the outputs spec, the `rxjs` 7.8.2 files, the `7.0.0` tag files, the `7.x` branch CHANGELOG and docs files, the WICG, MDN, webstatus.dev and Chrome pages with their dates, the Reactor 3.8.7 pages and the npm registry data; a script lists URLs in the module not in SOURCES (must print nothing).
  - Repeats: tell each mechanism once and link it afterwards; the notification contract (§2), cold versus hot (§3) and the completion rules of timing operators (§8) are the likeliest to be retold in questions, and answers link the section.
- **Marker regions:** a section's region is its marker line plus the six empty `###` headings under it. A question chunk's region is its marker line plus the question anchors and headings up to the next marker (QB-M22-01 also writes the bank intro at its placeholder). An exercise's region is its marker line, its `<a id>` and its heading (EX-M22-01 also writes the exercise intro; SEC-M22-01 writes the verification paragraph at its placeholder). CLOSE-M22's marker is in `## Summary`; the placeholders for Short on time, Check your understanding and Connections name CLOSE-M22, and the header's Labs line carries a relink comment for CLOSE-M22.
- **Prose budget** (deltas over the module count measured just before the task; M22.md "Budget"): skeleton 1,619 measured 2026-10-09 by `node labs/tools/module-stats.mjs` and the QUALITY-BAR python one-liner (equal). **Per-chunk caps, summing to the CLOSE limit:** SEC-M22-01..09 = 900 / 900 / 950 / 850 / 800 / 900 / 800 / 800 / 800 (7,700); EX-M22-01 = 560 (intro included), EX-M22-02..05 = 520 each (2,640); QB-M22-01..06 = 1,130 each and QB-M22-07 = 905 (7,685; QB-M22-01 includes the bank intro of at most 40 words); CLOSE-M22 = 800. 1,619 + 7,700 + 2,640 + 7,685 + 800 = 20,444. **CLOSE at most 20,450 words; REVIEW ceiling 21,350.** Each task measures before and after with both counters, puts both counts in its LOG line, and cuts before finishing if more than 5% over its delta (repeats first; never a diagram, a trap's cause or a "because"). Answer parts average at most 225 words per QB chunk, none above 290. If SEC-M22-09 ends with the sections more than 3% over 7,700, it writes EX and QB caps here so the module still closes at 20,450.
- Do not repeat what another module owns (M22.md "Owned elsewhere" and the 22/23 split): promises and `AbortController` 04; generics 07; the `async` pipe 14; `toSignal`/`toObservable`/`rxResource` and `BehaviorSubject` migration 17; `output()` and the Subject bus 19; `takeUntilDestroyed` 20; what an emission does to change detection 21; higher-order mapping, errors, `shareReplay`, schedulers, unsubscription and marble authoring 23; `Router.events` 24; `valueChanges` 25; `HttpClient` 27; testing async code 29.
- Module file: replace only the task's own `<!-- TASK: <ID> -->` region, at the end of the task, in a single edit. `<!-- PLACEHOLDER: … -->` lines are replaced by the task named in them. Never touch another task's marker. Labs: only the files M22.md "Lab files" assigns to the task, under `labs/angular/src/app/modules/22-rxjs-foundations/` (nothing under `labs/ts-js`). No test needs the internet. Throwaway probes live only in a scratch directory and are deleted before the task ends.

### Module 23 tasks (PLAN-M23) · common rules
- Inputs for every task: `_meta/modules/M23.md` (its section, question or exercise rows, the budget, "Lab decision", "Facts established while planning" F1-F33, "Could not be read" C1-C13, the owner map), STYLE-GUIDE §3-§8, QUALITY-BAR B-E, SYLLABUS §4 (23), the module skeleton, the pilot `modules/17-signals.md` and the finished `modules/21-change-detection.md` and `modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md` for Part D depth (read a section, answer or exercise of the same kind before writing; never copy), and `_meta/reviews/REVIEW-M22.md` and `REVIEW-M21.md` § Findings and § Proposed FIX tasks. Re-verify every fact the task uses before writing (lab > package source > official docs/changelog) and record the basis next to the claim; every item under "Could not be read" for the task's section is read or probed first, or the claim is dropped. The facts of M23.md were probed 2026-10-09 on `rxjs` 7.8.2 and Angular 22.2.1 (Node 24.21.0; plain RxJS with `node`, Angular under `ng test` in a scratch copy of `labs/angular`); a task re-runs the ones it quotes under `ng test`.
- **What runs and what is documented** (M23.md "Lab decision"): **every runtime claim is asserted by specs in `labs/angular` under `ng test`** (jsdom; `rxjs` 7.8.2, `rxjs/testing` `TestScheduler.run`, `vi.useFakeTimers`, `TestBed` with `provideHttpClientTesting`). No task touches `labs/ts-js`, any `package.json`, `angular.json` or a lockfile. Source reads name the file and function (`grep` first, in `labs/angular/node_modules/rxjs/dist/esm/internal`, `@angular/core/fesm2022/rxjs-interop.mjs`, `@angular/common/fesm2022/_module-chunk.mjs`). **Documented, never worded as observed:** real network cancellation (C5), browser scheduling, rAF against paint and page visibility (C4), memory and DevTools (C3), `pendingUntilEvent` behavior (C2), Angular docs sentences (from `main`, C1), RxJS 9. Scratch only under `mktemp -d` outside the repo, deleted at the end. Probes that need output under `ng test` collect it and fail an assertion to print it, in a scratch copy only. Specs assert tables of values and frames, never wall-clock durations; no `console.log`; fake timers restored in `afterEach`; error classes asserted by `name`, Angular errors by their `NGxxxx` code. Each spec builds its own `TestScheduler` inline; a non-spec file (fixtures, `counting-source.ts`, exercise sources) is compiled by `ng build`: no `vi`, no `expect`. An unbounded source inside `TestScheduler.run` is ended by an unsubscription marble, `take` or `maxFrames` (C10). Module 22's `concepts/support/timeline.ts` may be imported, never edited.
- **Corrections to apply from the plan (they override anything older):** status markers only in the STYLE-GUIDE §3.2 forms at the **first body mention in the module**, plain, with the versions of M23.md "Sections" (RxJS facts carry RxJS versions, Angular facts Angular versions); `flatMap` gets no version (C7); a deprecation is dated only where the typings or CHANGELOG of M23.md date it, and "will be removed in v8" is quoted as the typings' wording, never as a v8 that shipped. **Name the 22/23 split** (M23.md header): cold/hot, `share()` defaults, Subjects, the operator catalogue, combination and timing semantics, `take`/`first`/`takeUntil` as filters and `tap` are module 22: one sentence plus a link each, never retold. **Name the ownership of 20 and 23:** 20 owns the `takeUntilDestroyed` mechanism and NG0203 (link [20 §5](../modules/20-lifecycle-and-render-hooks.md#5-teardown-ngondestroy-destroyref-and-takeuntildestroyed)); 23 owns the comparison of strategies; 17 owns interop essentials and resources (link 17 §6 and §8), 23 goes deeper only where M23.md says. Every internal name of C13 is **marked internal at its first use in each section**. The RxJS 7.x docs statements of C6 are not repeated. No rationale, roadmap or "why the team chose" claim (C9).
- **Angular code** follows STYLE-GUIDE §7.1 (standalone, `inject()`, signals, signal inputs/outputs, built-in control flow, v22 file names, `protected`/`readonly`, no `any`) and RUN.md §10 (at most 4 injected dependencies per class). **Legacy code** lives only under a `legacy/` folder or a `#### Legacy` heading, says what it is, and compiles under `ng build`. Fixtures use the `lab-m23-` prefix and are standalone.
- **First-time-pass rules (from REVIEW-M14 to M22; the REVIEW-M21 and REVIEW-M22 findings are named so they do not recur):**
  - Factual slips: "which value", "at which frame", "first versus last", "before versus after complete" and "how many subscriptions" statements are checked against the recorded runs (F1-F33) before writing; every number in prose is traced to a test, run or read. **Do not repeat REVIEW-M22 F-1:** counting producer runs tells unicast from multicast, not cold from hot (link 22 §3 for the definitions). **F-2:** quote combinator and operator semantics with their exact completion and error rules (`race` follows the first notification, an error included; `concat` waits for completion).
  - Wording of observation (REVIEW-M22 F-4, T-6; REVIEW-M21): every "in the lab, X" names a test (`Section N: …`, `Q23.NN …`, `E23.N …`) asserting exactly X; no "not run" for something a test now asserts; no unrecorded run ("the run hung", "it crashed"); no unmeasured comparison ("less code", "twice as fast", "uses more memory"); a measured comparison names the count.
  - Quotes (REVIEW-M21): a quoted sentence is verbatim from the named file, branch and date; a shortened runtime message carries "(abridged)"; an error name or code is the one the source or the run gives, attributed to that source, not to the docs; a default is attached to the symbol that declares it (`refCount` is `shareReplay`'s, `resetOnRefCountZero` is `share`'s).
  - Header and history (REVIEW-M22 F-3, T-7; REVIEW-M21): the header lists only what the module teaches and cites; no RxJS 9 line; history sentences are dated from the CHANGELOG or typings only.
  - Markers (REVIEW-M21 C4): the marker sits at the **first body mention in the module**, not in a later section or in the question bank; a glossary entry never dates a deprecation the sources do not date (REVIEW-M22 F-7).
  - Owner credit (REVIEW-M22 F-8): name the owning module correctly in every link sentence (the `takeUntilDestroyed` mechanism is 20's, the strategy comparison 23's; flattening operators are 23's; `share()` defaults 22's; `toSignal` basics 17's).
  - Mermaid: every section has a diagram of the type named in M23.md followed by a line starting `What to notice`; quote every label with brackets, colons, `@`, `*`, `[` or parentheses; parse each diagram with the mermaid parser before finishing.
  - Misconceptions: **every** bullet gives the correction **and a cause clause**; "Once true: … until …" only from a source read in the task.
  - Terms (REVIEW-M22 T-3, REVIEW-M21 B9): define at first use with an owner link (M23.md lists the terms per section); run a first-use script over the module before finishing.
  - Code (REVIEW-M22 T-4, T-5; REVIEW-M21): each `### Code` subsection has numbered steps (QUALITY-BAR B5); a snippet shows what its caption claims, its title matches its code, and it shows every helper it calls (no hidden helper); sections 1, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8 and 9 have a `#### Legacy` subsection as M23.md says.
  - Mechanism: "How it actually works" answers at least one "why does it behave like that" from a source read, marked "read from the `rxjs` 7.8.2 source" (or `@angular/core` / `@angular/common` 22.2.1) with file and function named (each name found by `grep` in the named file), and "read, not run" where no test asserts it.
  - Titles and criteria (REVIEW-M21): a question title counts what the answer gives; Output only where a spec asserts the answer exactly; Output short answers give the result **and the reason** (REVIEW-M22 T-2); an exercise criterion's text equals its test title; an alternative offered in an exercise ("or …") passes the same tests or is removed (REVIEW-M22 F-5); a marker or placeholder replacement uses only what the typings give (REVIEW-M22 F-6).
  - Links (REVIEW-M22 T-1, T-8, T-10): questions linked from their section with their own `[Q23.NN](#q23-NN)`; every link to another module's question is checked against that question's heading before writing (the plan's owner map names them; debounce versus throttle is [Q02.19](../modules/02-js-scope-closures-this.md#q02-19)); links to other modules carry a section or question anchor, not the bare file; anchors are generated from the heading text by the GitHub rule (`check-links.mjs` `githubSlug`); `<!-- relink: … -->` only for a target not in the file yet; callouts begin `> [!NOTE]`/`> [!TIP]`/`> [!WARNING]`; a *Coming from the backend* callout is optional (C8) and, if written, reads the Reactor sources first and states **Where the analogy breaks**.
  - Narrative voice (REVIEW-M21): no first person ("we ran", "I found"); "the lab test … shows".
  - Format: a blank line before every `###` heading and before a list that follows a bold label; no doubled commas; no "SYLLABUS" in reader text; exercise Constraints list what is not covered and only asserted behaviors are criteria.
  - SOURCES: every URL and package read gets a line in `SOURCES.md`, including code-source lines for each exercise and the outputs spec; a script lists URLs in the module not in SOURCES (must print nothing).
  - Repeats (REVIEW-M22 T-9, REVIEW-M21): tell each mechanism once and link it afterwards; the four-operator semantics (§1), `catchError` placement (§3), the share resets (§5) and `takeUntil` placement (§6) are the likeliest to be retold in questions and exercises, and those link the section; no retelling of 22's or 17's material.
- **Marker regions:** a section's region is its marker line plus the six empty `###` headings under it. A question chunk's region is its marker line plus the question anchors and headings up to the next marker (QB-M23-01 also writes the bank intro at its placeholder). An exercise's region is its marker line, its `<a id>` and its heading (EX-M23-01 also writes the exercise intro; SEC-M23-01 writes the verification paragraph at its placeholder). CLOSE-M23's marker is in `## Summary`; the placeholders for Short on time, Check your understanding and Connections name CLOSE-M23, and the header's Labs line carries a relink comment for CLOSE-M23.
- **Prose budget** (deltas over the module count measured just before the task; M23.md "Budget"): skeleton 1,916 measured 2026-10-09 by `node labs/tools/module-stats.mjs` and the QUALITY-BAR python one-liner (equal). **Per-chunk caps, summing to the CLOSE limit:** SEC-M23-01..10 = 900 / 800 / 850 / 750 / 850 / 800 / 900 / 850 / 750 / 850 (8,300); EX-M23-01 = 560 (intro included), EX-M23-02..06 = 520 each (3,160); QB-M23-01..05 = 1,130 each, QB-M23-06 and -07 = 904 each, QB-M23-08 = 678 (8,136; QB-M23-01 includes the bank intro of at most 40 words); CLOSE-M23 = 800. 1,916 + 8,300 + 3,160 + 8,136 + 800 = 22,312. **CLOSE at most 22,320 words; REVIEW ceiling 23,220.** Each task measures before and after with both counters, puts both counts in its LOG line, and cuts before finishing if more than 5% over its delta (repeats first; never a diagram, a trap's cause or a "because"). Answer parts average at most 226 words per QB chunk, none above 290. If SEC-M23-10 ends with the sections more than 3% over 8,300, it writes EX and QB caps here so the module still closes at 22,320. **Rebalanced 2026-10-10 (main, after QB-M23-05 at 19,276):** sections, exercises and QB-01..05 ran +2-5% each, so the remaining caps are QB-M23-06 = 830, QB-M23-07 = 810 (set by main at the QB-M23-07 run: the module stood at 20,128 and QB-M23-06 ran +22 over its cap), QB-M23-08 = 600, CLOSE-M23 = 759 (set by main after QB-M23-07: the module stood at 20,961; 20,961 + 600 + 759 = 22,320); these override the per-chunk caps above and in the task entries.
- Do not repeat what another module owns (M23.md "Owned elsewhere"): promises and `AbortController` 04; debounce versus throttle 02 and 22; the `async` pipe 14; resources and interop essentials 17; `output()` 19; the `takeUntilDestroyed` mechanism 20; `PendingTasks` 21; the RxJS foundations 22; `Router.events` 24; `valueChanges` 25; `HttpClient` 27; state libraries 28; testing async code 29; WebSockets 43.
- Module file: replace only the task's own `<!-- TASK: <ID> -->` region, at the end of the task, in a single edit. `<!-- PLACEHOLDER: … -->` lines are replaced by the task named in them. Never touch another task's marker. Labs: only the files M23.md "Lab files" assigns to the task, under `labs/angular/src/app/modules/23-rxjs-in-depth/` (nothing under `labs/ts-js`; module 22's lab files read-only). No test needs the internet. Throwaway probes live only in a scratch directory and are deleted before the task ends.

### Module 24 tasks (PLAN-M24) · common rules
- Inputs for every task: `_meta/modules/M24.md` (its section, question or exercise rows, the budget, "Lab decision", "Facts established while planning" F1-F34, "Could not be read" C1-C13, the owner map), STYLE-GUIDE §3-§8, QUALITY-BAR B-E, SYLLABUS §4 (24), the module skeleton, the pilot `modules/17-signals.md` and the finished `modules/22-rxjs-foundations.md` and `modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md` for Part D depth (read a section, answer or exercise of the same kind before writing; never copy), and `_meta/reviews/REVIEW-M23.md` and `REVIEW-M22.md` § Findings and § Proposed FIX tasks. Re-verify every fact the task uses before writing (lab > package source > official docs/changelog) and record the basis next to the claim; every item under "Could not be read" for the task's section is read or probed first, or the claim is dropped. The facts of M24.md were probed 2026-10-10 on Angular 22.2.1 (`@angular/router` 22.2.1, `rxjs` 7.8.2, Node 24.21.0, jsdom 30.1.1) under `ng test` in a scratch copy of `labs/angular`; a task re-runs the ones it quotes under `ng test`. Where M24.md says "probe first", the run wins over the plan: write what the run shows and state the difference in the LOG line.
- **What runs and what is documented** (M24.md "Lab decision"): **every runtime claim is asserted by specs in `labs/angular` under `ng test`** (jsdom; `RouterTestingHarness` from `@angular/router/testing`; `TestBed.inject(ApplicationRef).bootstrap(Root, el)` for preloading and in-memory scrolling, which the harness does not start (F27); a `document.startViewTransition` stub for view transitions, deleted in `finally` (F31)). No task touches `labs/ts-js`, any `package.json`, `angular.json` or a lockfile. Source reads name the file and function (`grep` first, in `labs/angular/node_modules/@angular/router/fesm2022/_router-chunk.mjs`, `_router_module-chunk.mjs`, `types/router.d.ts`, `types/_router_module-chunk.d.ts`, and `@angular/common/fesm2022/_location-chunk.mjs`, `_common_module-chunk.mjs`). **Documented, never worded as observed:** browser back/forward and scroll restore (C3), the View Transitions animation (C4), the Navigation API integration (C5), server deep-link configuration (C6), network-aware preloading (C7), `nonBlocking` until SEC-M24-06's probe (C9), Angular docs sentences (from `main`, C1). Scratch only under `mktemp -d` outside the repo, deleted at the end. Probes that need output under `ng test` collect it and fail an assertion to print it, in a scratch copy only. Specs assert URLs, event classes (by `instanceof`), cancellation codes, call counts and instance identities, never wall-clock durations (an async guard's `timer(30)` is input); no `console.log`; every bootstrapped root destroyed and its element removed in `finally`; Angular errors asserted by their `NGxxxx` code. A spec that renders `<router-outlet>` imports `RouterOutlet`; a spec with a second router configuration calls `TestBed.resetTestingModule()` first. A non-spec file (fixtures, exercise sources, `legacy/`) is compiled by `ng build`: no `vi`, no `expect`.
- **Corrections to apply from the plan (they override anything older):** status markers only in the STYLE-GUIDE §3.2 forms at the **first body mention in the module**, plain, with the versions of M24.md "Sections"; `withExperimentalAutoCleanupInjectors` gets no version (C8); `withViewTransitions` is `[Developer preview since v19.0]` (typings) with the 17.0 `enableViewTransitions` history in the legacy line; a deprecation is dated only where the typings or CHANGELOG of M24.md date it. **Name the 15/16/24 split** (M24.md header): `loadComponent` versus `loadChildren`, default-export unwrapping, `NG04014` for `loadComponent` given a module and `forRoot` versus `provideRouter` are 15's; route-injector identities and the injector tree are 16's; 24 owns loader call counts, `canMatch` before the loader, route-injector lifetime and auto cleanup. One sentence plus a link each, never retold. **Guards are UX, not security:** the only security claim is the docs' CRITICAL line of F20 (quoted verbatim with path, branch `main` and date) plus links to 33 and 42 (C10). Every internal name of C11 is **marked internal at its first use in each section**. No rationale, roadmap or "why the team chose" claim.
- **Angular code** follows STYLE-GUIDE §7.1 (standalone, `inject()`, signals, signal inputs/outputs, built-in control flow, functional guards and resolvers, v22 file names, `protected`/`readonly`, no `any`) and RUN.md §10 (at most 4 injected dependencies per class). **Legacy code** lives only under a `legacy/` folder or a `#### Legacy` heading, says what it is, and compiles under `ng build`. Fixtures use the `lab-m24-` prefix (selectors) and `LabM24…` class names and are standalone.
- **First-time-pass rules (from REVIEW-M14 to M23; named so they do not recur):**
  - Factual slips: "which URL", "which event order", "which code", "how many loader calls", "same instance or new" and "which value binds" statements are checked against the recorded runs (F1-F33) before writing; every number in prose is traced to a test, run or read. A navigation that is cancelled **resolves `false`**; only an error **rejects** (F21, F24); a redirect resolves `true` after a second `NavigationStart` (F16).
  - Wording of observation: every "in the lab, X" names a test (`Section N: …`, `Q24.NN …`, `E24.N …`) asserting exactly X; no "not run" for something a test now asserts; no unrecorded run; no unmeasured comparison ("faster", "uses more memory"); a measured comparison names the count.
  - Quotes: a quoted sentence is verbatim from the named file, branch and date; a shortened runtime message carries "(abridged)"; an error code is the one the run gives (`NG04002`, `NG04014`, `NG04016`), attributed to the run or the source, not to the docs; a default is attached to the symbol that declares it (`paramsInheritanceStrategy` and `onSameUrlNavigation` are `RouterConfigOptions`'; `unmatchedInputBehavior` is `ComponentInputBindingOptions`').
  - Header and history: the header lists only what the module teaches and cites; history sentences are dated from the CHANGELOG or typings only.
  - Markers: the marker sits at the **first body mention in the module**, not in a later section or in the question bank; a glossary entry never dates a deprecation the sources do not date.
  - Owner credit: name the owning module correctly in every link sentence (lazy loading basics 15's, injector identities 16's, History versus Navigation API 08's, focus after navigation 11's, resources 17's, signal inputs 19's, `switchMap` 23's).
  - Mermaid: every section has a diagram of the type named in M24.md followed by a line starting `What to notice`; quote every label with brackets, colons, `@`, `*`, `[`, `/`, `?`, `#` or parentheses (route paths and URLs always); parse each diagram with the mermaid parser before finishing.
  - Misconceptions: **every** bullet gives the correction **and a cause clause**; "Once true: … until …" only from a source read in the task.
  - Terms: define at first use with an owner link (M24.md lists the terms per section); run a first-use script over the module before finishing.
  - Code: each `### Code` subsection has numbered steps (QUALITY-BAR B5); a snippet shows what its caption claims, its title matches its code, and it shows every helper and the full route table it uses (no hidden helper); sections 1, 2, 5, 6, 7, 8 and 9 have a `#### Legacy` subsection as M24.md says.
  - Mechanism: "How it actually works" answers at least one "why does it behave like that" from a source read, marked "read from the `@angular/router` 22.2.1 source" (or `@angular/common` 22.2.1) with file and function named (each name found by `grep` in the named file), and "read, not run" where no test asserts it.
  - Titles and criteria: a question title counts what the answer gives; Output only where a spec asserts the answer exactly; Output short answers give the result **and the reason**; an exercise criterion's text equals its test title; an alternative offered in an exercise ("or …") passes the same tests or is removed; a marker or placeholder replacement uses only what the typings give.
  - Links: questions linked from their section with their own `[Q24.NN](#q24-NN)`; every link to another module's question is checked against that question's heading before writing (M24.md "Owned elsewhere" names them); links to other modules carry a section or question anchor, not the bare file; anchors are generated from the heading text by the GitHub rule (`check-links.mjs` `githubSlug`); `<!-- relink: … -->` only for a target not in the file yet; callouts begin `> [!NOTE]`/`> [!TIP]`/`> [!WARNING]`; a *Coming from the backend* callout is optional (C2) and, if written, reads docs.spring.io first and states **Where the analogy breaks**.
  - Narrative voice: no first person ("we ran", "I found"); "the lab test … shows".
  - Format: a blank line before every `###` heading and before a list that follows a bold label; no doubled commas; no "SYLLABUS" in reader text; exercise Constraints list what is not covered and only asserted behaviors are criteria.
  - SOURCES: every URL and package file read gets a line in `SOURCES.md`, including code-source lines for each exercise and the outputs spec; a script lists URLs in the module not in SOURCES (must print nothing).
  - Repeats: tell each mechanism once and link it afterwards; the matching order (§1), the input-binding merge (§2), the guard order and array combination (§5), the resolver outcomes (§6) and the event list and cancellation codes (§7) are the likeliest to be retold in questions and exercises, and those link the section.
- **Marker regions:** a section's region is its marker line plus the six empty `###` headings under it. A question chunk's region is its marker line plus the question anchors and headings up to the next marker (QB-M24-01 also writes the bank intro at its placeholder). An exercise's region is its marker line, its `<a id>` and its heading (EX-M24-01 also writes the exercise intro; SEC-M24-01 writes the verification paragraph at its placeholder). CLOSE-M24's marker is in `## Summary`; the placeholders for Short on time, Check your understanding and Connections name CLOSE-M24, and the header's Labs line carries a relink comment for CLOSE-M24.
- **Prose budget** (deltas over the module count measured just before the task; M24.md "Budget"): skeleton 1,879 measured 2026-10-10 by `node labs/tools/module-stats.mjs` and the QUALITY-BAR python one-liner (equal). **Per-chunk caps, summing to the CLOSE limit:** SEC-M24-01..10 = 900 / 900 / 700 / 750 / 900 / 850 / 900 / 800 / 750 / 700 (8,150); EX-M24-01 = 560 (intro included), EX-M24-02..06 = 520 each (3,160); QB-M24-01..06 = 1,130 each, QB-M24-07 = 904 (7,684; QB-M24-01 includes the bank intro of at most 40 words); CLOSE-M24 = 800. 1,879 + 8,150 + 3,160 + 7,684 + 800 = 21,673. **CLOSE at most 21,680 words; REVIEW ceiling 22,580.** Each task measures before and after with both counters, puts both counts in its LOG line, and cuts before finishing if more than 5% over its delta (repeats first; never a diagram, a trap's cause or a "because"). Answer parts average at most 226 words per QB chunk, none above 290. If SEC-M24-10 ends with the sections more than 3% over 8,150, it writes EX and QB caps here so the module still closes at 21,680.
- Do not repeat what another module owns (M24.md "Owned elsewhere"): `import()` 05; `ResolveFn` typing 07; History versus Navigation API 08; focus and announcements after navigation 11; chunks 12; lazy loading basics and `forRoot` 15; the injector tree and route-injector identities 16; resources 17; `@defer` chunks 18; signal inputs 19; teardown 20; `switchMap` and unsubscription strategies 23; testing 29; performance 31; SSR and initial navigation 32; security 33; localized routes 34; large-app routing 38; login flows 42.
- Module file: replace only the task's own `<!-- TASK: <ID> -->` region, at the end of the task, in a single edit. `<!-- PLACEHOLDER: … -->` lines are replaced by the task named in them. Never touch another task's marker. Labs: only the files M24.md "Lab files" assigns to the task, under `labs/angular/src/app/modules/24-routing/` (nothing under `labs/ts-js`; other modules' lab files read-only). No test needs the internet. Throwaway probes live only in a scratch directory and are deleted before the task ends.
- Every task adds its sources under `## Module 24 · Routing` in `SOURCES.md` (after the last module section present; package reads as `<pkg>@<version>, <file>, read <date>`) and its inline-defined terms under `## Module 24` in `_meta/GLOSSARY-TERMS.md`.
- Acceptance for every task: the module's lab tests pass (`labs/angular`: `npx ng test --watch=false --include='src/app/modules/24-routing/**/*.spec.ts'` and `npx tsc -p tsconfig.spec.json --noEmit`, plus `npx ng build` when a non-spec file changed, which also compiles the `legacy/` samples); module 23's lab tests still pass; `node labs/tools/check-snippets.mjs . 24` and `node labs/tools/check-links.mjs --planned` exit 0; every URL in the module is in SOURCES.md; only the declared files are new or changed (`git status --short`); the task's marker is gone; no scratch or probe file is left behind; the LOG line gives the part's and the module's prose count.

### Module 25 tasks (PLAN-M25) · common rules
- Inputs for every task: `_meta/modules/M25.md` (its section, question or exercise rows, the budget, "Signal Forms handoff", "Lab decision", "Facts established while planning" F1-F24, "Could not be read" C1-C12, the owner map), STYLE-GUIDE §3-§8, QUALITY-BAR B-E, SYLLABUS §4 (25), the module skeleton, the pilot `modules/17-signals.md` and the finished `modules/23-rxjs-in-depth.md` and `modules/24-routing.md` for Part D depth (read a section, answer or exercise of the same kind before writing; never copy), and `_meta/reviews/REVIEW-M24.md` and `REVIEW-M23.md` § Findings and § Proposed FIX tasks. Re-verify every fact the task uses before writing (lab > package source > official docs/changelog) and record the basis next to the claim; every item under "Could not be read" for the task's section is read or probed first, or the claim is dropped. The facts of M25.md were probed 2026-10-10 on Angular 22.2.1 (`@angular/forms` 22.2.1, `rxjs` 7.8.2, Node 24.21.0, jsdom 30.1.1) under `ng test` and `tsc -p tsconfig.spec.json` in a scratch copy of `labs/angular`; a task re-runs the ones it quotes under `ng test`. Where M25.md says "probe first", the run wins over the plan: write what the run shows and state the difference in the LOG line.
- **What runs and what is documented** (M25.md "Lab decision"): **every runtime claim is asserted by specs in `labs/angular` under `ng test`** (jsdom; zoneless `TestBed`; model-only claims without a component; DOM claims by dispatching `input`/`blur` events on native inputs; template-driven specs await `fixture.whenStable()` (F2); zoneless rendering claims use `autoDetectChanges()` plus `whenStable()`, never `detectChanges()` (F18); async validators under `vi.useFakeTimers()` restored in `finally`; compile-time claims as `// @ts-expect-error` lines checked by `tsc -p tsconfig.spec.json`). No task touches `labs/ts-js`, any `package.json`, `angular.json` or a lockfile. Source reads name the file and function (`grep` first, in `labs/angular/node_modules/@angular/forms/fesm2022/forms.mjs` and `types/forms.d.ts`; `types/signals.d.ts` only for F9). **Documented, never worded as observed:** screen-reader output, IME composition, autofill, the native validation bubble and real focus behavior (C4), Signal Forms (C3, module 26), Angular docs sentences (from `main`, C1), the docs' "pending until complete" line (C5). Scratch only under `mktemp -d` outside the repo, deleted at the end. Probes that need output under `ng test` collect it and fail an assertion to print it, in a scratch copy only. Specs assert values, error maps, statuses, event classes (by `instanceof`), call logs and rendered text, never wall-clock durations (a `timer(300)` in a validator is input); no `console.log`; `console.warn` spies and fake timers restored in `finally`; Angular errors asserted by their `NGxxxxx` code. Every fixture component is standalone with a unique `lab-m25-` selector (the default `ng-component` selector logged `NG0912` collisions in the probe). A non-spec file (exercise sources, `legacy/`) is compiled by `ng build`: no `vi`, no `expect`.
- **Corrections to apply from the plan (they override anything older):** status markers only in the STYLE-GUIDE §3.2 forms at the **first body mention in the module**, plain, with the versions of M25.md "Sections"; **no `[Changed in v22]` marker on `Validators.min`/`max` or the `min`/`max` directives**: the v22 numeric-only change is Signal Forms' rules (F9), stated in §5 and Q25.16 with the CHANGELOG line and the typings, one sentence plus a link to 26; a deprecation is dated only where the typings or CHANGELOG of M25.md date it (C6). **Signal Forms handoff:** no `@angular/forms/signals` import in text or labs; only the sentences M25.md allows (C3), each linking [26](../modules/26-signal-forms.md) file-level. **Name the 07/11/13/16/25 split** (M25.md header): `value` versus `getRawValue()` and nullable versus `nonNullable` typing are 07's; accessible names, `aria-describedby`, WCAG and error summaries are 11's; `[(x)]` desugaring is 13's; `useExisting`, `viewProviders`, `multi` and `forwardRef` are 16's. One sentence plus a link each, never retold. Every internal name of C7 is **marked internal at its first use in each section**. No rationale, roadmap or "why the team chose" claim.
- **Angular code** follows STYLE-GUIDE §7.1 (standalone, `inject()`, signals, signal inputs/outputs, built-in control flow with `track`, v22 file names, `protected`/`readonly`, no `any`, no `as` outside a test of a type error) and RUN.md §10 (at most 4 injected dependencies per class). **Legacy code** lives only under a `legacy/` folder or a `#### Legacy` heading, says what it is, and compiles under `ng build`. Fixtures use the `lab-m25-` prefix (selectors) and `LabM25…` class names.
- **First-time-pass rules (from REVIEW-M14 to M24; named so they do not recur):**
  - Factual slips: "which value", "which error map", "which status", "which event order", "how many calls" and "does it repaint" statements are checked against the recorded runs (F2-F23) before writing; every number in prose is traced to a test, run or read. A status flip repaints a zoneless view and a value-only `setValue` does not (F18); a single never-completing async validator settles and two do not (F14); a group validator's error is on the group (F10).
  - Wording of observation: every "in the lab, X" names a test (`Section N: …`, `Q25.NN …`, `E25.N …`) asserting exactly X; no "not run" for something a test now asserts; no unrecorded run; no unmeasured comparison ("faster", "fewer requests" without a count); a measured comparison names the count.
  - Quotes: a quoted sentence is verbatim from the named file, branch and date; a shortened runtime message carries "(abridged)" (every `NG01xxx` message ends with a docs URL, C12); an error code is the one the run gives (`NG01001`, `NG01002`, `NG01200`, `NG01203`, `NG01354`), attributed to the run or the source, not to the docs; a default is attached to the symbol that declares it (`updateOn` `'change'` is `AbstractControlOptions`'; `callSetDisabledState` `'always'` is the forms modules' config).
  - Header and history: the header lists only what the module teaches and cites; history sentences are dated from the CHANGELOG or typings only.
  - Markers: the marker sits at the **first body mention in the module**, not in a later section or in the question bank; a glossary entry never dates a deprecation the sources do not date.
  - Owner credit: name the owning module correctly in every link sentence (typed-forms typing 07's, accessible errors 11's, two-way binding 13's, banana diagnostic 12's, `multi`/`forwardRef`/`useExisting`/`viewProviders` 16's, `model()` 17's and 19's, the zoneless scheduler 21's, timing operators 22's, `switchMap`/`toSignal`/typeahead 23's, unsaved-changes guard 24's, Signal Forms 26's).
  - Mermaid: every section has a diagram of the type named in M25.md followed by a line starting `What to notice`; quote every label with brackets, colons, `@`, `*`, `[`, `/`, `?`, `#`, parentheses or `=`; parse each diagram with the mermaid parser before finishing.
  - Misconceptions: **every** bullet gives the correction **and a cause clause**; "Once true: … until …" only from a source read in the task.
  - Terms: define at first use with an owner link (M25.md lists the terms per section); run a first-use script over the module before finishing.
  - Code: each `### Code` subsection has numbered steps (QUALITY-BAR B5); a snippet shows what its caption claims, its title matches its code, and it shows every helper and the full template it uses (no hidden helper); sections 1, 2, 3, 4, 7 and 10 have a `#### Legacy` subsection as M25.md says.
  - Mechanism: "How it actually works" answers at least one "why does it behave like that" from a source read, marked "read from the `@angular/forms` 22.2.1 source" with file and function named (each name found by `grep` in the named file), and "read, not run" where no test asserts it.
  - Titles and criteria: a question title counts what the answer gives; Output only where a spec asserts the answer exactly; Output short answers give the result **and the reason**; an exercise criterion's text equals its test title; an alternative offered in an exercise ("or …") passes the same tests or is removed; a marker or placeholder replacement uses only what the typings give.
  - Links: questions linked from their section with their own `[Q25.NN](#q25-NN)`; every link to another module's question is checked against that question's heading before writing (M25.md "Owned elsewhere" names them); links to other modules carry a section or question anchor, not the bare file (26, 27, 29, 33, 36, 41 are planned files: file-level); anchors are generated from the heading text by the GitHub rule (`check-links.mjs` `githubSlug`); `<!-- relink: … -->` only for a target not in the file yet; callouts begin `> [!NOTE]`/`> [!TIP]`/`> [!WARNING]`; a *Coming from the backend* callout is optional (C2) and, if written, reads the Jakarta Validation or Hibernate Validator source first and states **Where the analogy breaks**.
  - Narrative voice: no first person ("we ran", "I found"); "the lab test … shows".
  - Format: a blank line before every `###` heading and before a list that follows a bold label; no doubled commas; no "SYLLABUS" in reader text; exercise Constraints list what is not covered and only asserted behaviors are criteria.
  - SOURCES: every URL and package file read gets a line in `SOURCES.md`, including code-source lines for each exercise and the outputs spec; a script lists URLs in the module not in SOURCES (must print nothing).
  - Repeats: tell each mechanism once and link it afterwards; the template-driven microtask (§4), the built-in validator table and the `min`/`max` truth (§5), the async cancellation (§6), the event order and the zoneless repaint rule (§7) and the CVA call order (§10) are the likeliest to be retold in questions and exercises, and those link the section.
- **Marker regions:** a section's region is its marker line plus the six empty `###` headings under it. A question chunk's region is its marker line plus the question anchors and headings up to the next marker (QB-M25-01 also writes the bank intro at its placeholder). An exercise's region is its marker line, its `<a id>` and its heading (EX-M25-01 also writes the exercise intro; SEC-M25-01 writes the verification paragraph at its placeholder). CLOSE-M25's marker is in `## Summary`; the placeholders for Short on time, Check your understanding and Connections name CLOSE-M25, and the header's Labs line carries a relink comment for CLOSE-M25.
- **Prose budget** (deltas over the module count measured just before the task; M25.md "Budget"): skeleton 1,907 measured 2026-10-10 by `node labs/tools/module-stats.mjs` and the QUALITY-BAR python one-liner (equal). **Per-chunk caps, summing to the CLOSE limit:** SEC-M25-01..10 = 900 / 800 / 700 / 800 / 900 / 800 / 900 / 750 / 800 / 850 (8,200); EX-M25-01 = 560 (intro included), EX-M25-02..06 = 520 each (3,160); QB-M25-01..06 = 1,130 each, QB-M25-07 = 904 (7,684; QB-M25-01 includes the bank intro of at most 40 words); CLOSE-M25 = 800. 1,907 + 8,200 + 3,160 + 7,684 + 800 = 21,751. **CLOSE at most 21,760 words; REVIEW ceiling 22,660.** Each task measures before and after with both counters, puts both counts in its LOG line, and cuts before finishing if more than 5% over its delta (repeats first; never a diagram, a trap's cause or a "because"). Answer parts average at most 226 words per QB chunk, none above 290. If SEC-M25-10 ends with the sections more than 3% over 8,200, it writes EX and QB caps here so the module still closes at 21,760.
- Do not repeat what another module owns (M25.md "Owned elsewhere"): typed-forms typing 07; accessible errors and focus 11; banana diagnostic 12; two-way binding 13; `useExisting`, `viewProviders`, `multi`, `forwardRef` 16; `model()` 17 and 19; the zoneless scheduler 21; timing operators 22; `switchMap`, `toSignal` and the typeahead 23; the unsaved-changes guard 24; Signal Forms 26; `HttpClient` 27; testing 29; server validation 33 and 41; Material form fields 36.
- Module file: replace only the task's own `<!-- TASK: <ID> -->` region, at the end of the task, in a single edit. `<!-- PLACEHOLDER: … -->` lines are replaced by the task named in them. Never touch another task's marker. Labs: only the files M25.md "Lab files" assigns to the task, under `labs/angular/src/app/modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven/` (nothing under `labs/ts-js`; other modules' lab files read-only). No test needs the internet. Throwaway probes live only in a scratch directory and are deleted before the task ends.
- Every task adds its sources under `## Module 25 · Forms: reactive and template-driven` in `SOURCES.md` (after the last module section present; package reads as `<pkg>@<version>, <file>, read <date>`) and its inline-defined terms under `## Module 25` in `_meta/GLOSSARY-TERMS.md`.

### Module 26 tasks (PLAN-M26) · common rules
- Inputs for every task: `_meta/modules/M26.md` (its section, question or exercise rows, the budget, "Module 25 handoff", "Lab decision", "Facts established while planning" F1-F29, "Could not be read" C1-C12, the owner map), STYLE-GUIDE §3-§8, QUALITY-BAR B-E, SYLLABUS §4 (26), the module skeleton, the pilot `modules/17-signals.md` and the finished `modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md` and `modules/24-routing.md` for Part D depth (read a section, answer or exercise of the same kind before writing; never copy), and `_meta/reviews/REVIEW-M25.md` and `REVIEW-M24.md` § Findings and § Proposed FIX tasks. Re-verify every fact the task uses before writing (lab > package source > official docs/changelog) and record the basis next to the claim; every item under "Could not be read" for the task's section is read or probed first, or the claim is dropped. The facts of M26.md were probed 2026-10-11 on Angular 22.2.1 (`@angular/forms` 22.2.1, Node 24.21.0) under `ng test` and `tsc -p tsconfig.spec.json` in a scratch copy of `labs/angular`; a task re-runs the ones it quotes under `ng test`. Where M26.md says "probe first", the run wins over the plan: write what the run shows and state the difference in the LOG line.
- **What runs and what is documented** (M26.md "Lab decision"): **every runtime claim is asserted by specs in `labs/angular` under `ng test`** (jsdom; zoneless `TestBed`; model-only claims inside `TestBed.runInInjectionContext`, because `form()` throws NG0203 elsewhere (F7); DOM claims by dispatching `input`/`blur`/cancelable `submit` events; rendering claims with `autoDetectChanges()` plus `whenStable()`, never `detectChanges()`; **fake timers only in model-only specs**, restored in `finally`, **never with `autoDetectChanges()`** (that probe hung until the 5,000 ms timeout, F12); compile-time claims as `// @ts-expect-error` lines checked by `tsc -p tsconfig.spec.json`, with the TypeScript code recorded). No task touches `labs/ts-js`, any `package.json`, `angular.json` or a lockfile; **no `zod` import** (transitive only, C3). Source reads name the file and function (`grep` first, in `labs/angular/node_modules/@angular/forms/types/{signals,_structure-chunk,signals-compat}.d.ts` and `fesm2022/{signals,_validation_errors-chunk,signals-compat}.mjs`). **Documented, never worded as observed:** screen-reader output and real focus, native pickers, WebMCP (not taught, C5), Angular docs sentences (from `main`, C1), cancellation until a test asserts it (F18), `validateHttp` unless its probe passes (C4). Scratch only under `mktemp -d` outside the repo, deleted at the end. Specs assert values, error kinds, statuses, call logs and rendered text, never wall-clock durations; no `console.log`; Angular errors by their `NGxxxx` code. Every fixture component is standalone with a unique `lab-m26-` selector. A non-spec file (exercise sources, `legacy/`) is compiled by `ng build`: no `vi`, no `expect`.
- **Corrections to apply from the plan (they override anything older):** status markers only in the STYLE-GUIDE §3.2 forms at the **first body mention in the module**, plain, with the versions of M26.md "Sections" (`[Stable since v22.0]` for Signal Forms, `[Added in v22.1]` `isFieldTree`, `[Changed in v22: numbers or null only]` `min`/`max`, `[Changed in v22: touched is an input and blur is reported by a touch output]`, `[Deprecated since v22.0 — use { when }]` for the function overloads, `[Added in v22.0]` `ngNoCva`); no marker on the other 22.0 APIs. **Module 25 handoff:** the v22 `min`/`max` rule, the custom-control contract and interop are taught here; reactive-forms mechanics are 25's and are linked, never retold. **Name the 16/17/25 split:** the injection context is 16's, `linkedSignal`, `resource` and `model()` are 17's, CVA, reactive async validators, error display and `Validators.min` are 25's. Every internal name of C12 is **marked internal at its first use in each section**. No rationale, roadmap or "why the team chose" claim.
- **Angular code** follows STYLE-GUIDE §7.1 (standalone, `inject()`, signals, signal inputs/outputs, built-in control flow with `track`, v22 file names, `protected`/`readonly`, no `any`, no `as` outside a test of a type error) and RUN.md §10 (at most 4 injected dependencies per class). Components import `FormField` (and `FormRoot`) from `@angular/forms/signals`; compat APIs from `@angular/forms/signals/compat`. **Legacy code** lives only under `legacy/` or a `#### Legacy` heading (§5, §8, §9), says what it is, and compiles under `ng build` (§9's v21 block is a `// Partial:` text block). Fixtures use the `lab-m26-` prefix and `LabM26…` class names.
- **First-time-pass rules (from REVIEW-M14 to M25; named so they do not recur):**
  - Factual slips: "which value", "which error kind", "which field holds the error", "pending, valid or invalid", "how many calls" and "does it repaint" statements are checked against the recorded runs (F6-F26) before writing; every number in prose is traced to a test, run or read. Root `errors()` is empty while `errorSummary()` lists descendants (F9); disabled, hidden and readonly fields report no errors (F14); a pending field is neither valid nor invalid (F17); a programmatic `value.set` does not dirty (F8); blur flushes a debounced value (F11).
  - Wording of observation: every "in the lab, X" names a test (`Section N: …`, `Q26.NN …`, `E26.N …`) asserting exactly X; no unrecorded run; no unmeasured comparison; a measured comparison names the count.
  - Quotes: a quoted sentence is verbatim from the named file, branch and date; a shortened runtime message carries "(abridged)"; an error code is the one the run gives (`NG0203`, the TypeScript code of F16), attributed to the run or the source; a default is attached to the symbol that declares it (`ignoreValidators` `'pending'` is `FormSubmitOptions`').
  - Header and history: the header lists only what the module teaches and cites; history sentences are dated from the CHANGELOG, typings tags or the `npm pack` reads of F2/F4 only; CHANGELOG lines above 22.2.1 are never cited as shipped (C1).
  - Markers: at the **first body mention in the module**, not in a later section or in the question bank; a glossary entry never dates what the sources do not date.
  - Owner credit: name the owning module correctly in every link sentence (injection context 16's, `linkedSignal`/`resource`/`model()` 17's, `model()` versus `input()` 19's, the zoneless scheduler 21's, timing operators 22's, `rxResource` and the typeahead 23's, CVA/reactive validators/error display/`Validators.min` 25's, accessibility 11's, two-way binding 13's, HTTP 27's, testing 29's).
  - Mermaid: every section has a diagram of the type named in M26.md followed by a line starting `What to notice`; quote every label with brackets, colons, `@`, `*`, `[`, `/`, `?`, `#`, parentheses or `=`; parse each diagram with the mermaid parser before finishing.
  - Misconceptions: **every** bullet gives the correction **and a cause clause**; "Once true: … until …" only from a source read in the task.
  - Terms: define at first use with an owner link (M26.md lists the terms per section); run a first-use script over the module before finishing.
  - Code: each `### Code` subsection has numbered steps (QUALITY-BAR B5); a snippet shows what its caption claims and every helper and the full template it uses; sections 5, 8 and 9 have a `#### Legacy` subsection as M26.md says.
  - Mechanism: "How it actually works" answers at least one "why does it behave like that" from a source read, marked "read from the `@angular/forms` 22.2.1 source" with file and function named (each name found by `grep` in the named file), and "read, not run" where no test asserts it.
  - Titles and criteria: a question title counts what the answer gives; Output only where a spec asserts the answer exactly; Output short answers give the result **and the reason**; an exercise criterion's text equals its test title; an alternative offered in an exercise passes the same tests or is removed.
  - Links: questions linked from their section with their own `[Q26.NN](#q26-NN)`; every link to another module's question is checked against that question's heading before writing; links to other modules carry a section or question anchor (27, 28, 29, 36 are planned files: file-level); anchors follow `check-links.mjs` `githubSlug`; `<!-- relink: … -->` only for a target not in the file yet; callouts begin `> [!NOTE]`/`> [!TIP]`/`> [!WARNING]`; a *Coming from the backend* callout is optional (C2) and, if written, reads the Jakarta Validation or Hibernate Validator source first and states **Where the analogy breaks**.
  - Narrative voice: no first person; "the lab test … shows".
  - Format: a blank line before every `###` heading and before a list that follows a bold label; no doubled commas; no "SYLLABUS" in reader text; exercise Constraints list what is not covered and only asserted behaviors are criteria.
  - SOURCES: every URL and package file read gets a line in `SOURCES.md`, including code-source lines for each exercise and the outputs spec; a script lists URLs in the module not in SOURCES (must print nothing).
  - Repeats: tell each mechanism once and link it afterwards; the model sync (§2), the skip rule for disabled/hidden/readonly (§5), the async resource mapping (§6), the submit protocol (§7) and the v21-to-v22 table (§9) are the likeliest to be retold in questions and exercises, and those link the section.
- **Marker regions:** a section's region is its marker line plus the six empty `###` headings under it. A question chunk's region is its marker line plus the question anchors and headings up to the next marker (QB-M26-01 also writes the bank intro at its placeholder). An exercise's region is its marker line, its `<a id>` and its heading (EX-M26-01 also writes the exercise intro; SEC-M26-01 writes the verification paragraph at its placeholder). CLOSE-M26's marker is in `## Summary`; the placeholders for Short on time, Check your understanding and Connections name CLOSE-M26, and the header's Labs line carries a relink comment for CLOSE-M26.
- **Prose budget** (deltas over the module count measured just before the task; M26.md "Budget"): skeleton 1,695 measured 2026-10-11 by `node labs/tools/module-stats.mjs` and the QUALITY-BAR python one-liner (equal). **Per-chunk caps, summing to the CLOSE limit:** SEC-M26-01..09 = 900 / 800 / 800 / 900 / 850 / 850 / 850 / 850 / 900 (7,700); EX-M26-01 = 560 (intro included), EX-M26-02..05 = 520 each (2,640); QB-M26-01..04 = 1,130 each, QB-M26-05..06 = 904 each (6,328; QB-M26-01 includes the bank intro of at most 40 words); CLOSE-M26 = 800. 1,695 + 7,700 + 2,640 + 6,328 + 800 = 19,163. **CLOSE at most 19,170 words; REVIEW ceiling 20,070.** Each task measures before and after with both counters, puts both counts in its LOG line, and cuts before finishing if more than 5% over its delta (repeats first; never a diagram, a trap's cause or a "because"). Answer parts average at most 226 words per QB chunk, none above 290.
- Do not repeat what another module owns (M26.md "Owned elsewhere"): accessible errors 11; two-way binding 13; the injection context 16; writable signals, `linkedSignal`, `resource`, `model()` and the reactive graph 17; `model()` versus `input()` 19; the zoneless scheduler 21; timing operators 22; `rxResource` and the typeahead 23; reactive and template-driven forms, CVA, reactive async validators, error display and `Validators.min` 25; `HttpClient` 27; form state as application state 28; testing 29; Material form fields 36.
- Module file: replace only the task's own `<!-- TASK: <ID> -->` region, at the end of the task, in a single edit. `<!-- PLACEHOLDER: … -->` lines are replaced by the task named in them. Never touch another task's marker. Labs: only the files M26.md "Lab files" assigns to the task, under `labs/angular/src/app/modules/26-signal-forms/` (nothing under `labs/ts-js`; other modules' lab files read-only). No test needs the internet. Throwaway probes live only in a scratch directory and are deleted before the task ends.
- Every task adds its sources under `## Module 26 · Signal Forms` in `SOURCES.md` (after the last module section present; package reads as `<pkg>@<version>, <file>, read <date>`) and its inline-defined terms under `## Module 26` in `_meta/GLOSSARY-TERMS.md`.
- Acceptance for every task: the module's lab tests pass (`labs/angular`: `npx ng test --watch=false --include='src/app/modules/26-signal-forms/**/*.spec.ts'` and `npx tsc -p tsconfig.spec.json --noEmit`, plus `npx ng build` when a non-spec file changed, which also compiles `legacy/`); module 25's lab tests still pass; `node labs/tools/check-snippets.mjs . 26` and `node labs/tools/check-links.mjs --planned` exit 0; every URL in the module is in SOURCES.md; only the declared files are new or changed (`git status --short`); the task's marker is gone; no scratch or probe file is left behind; the LOG line gives the part's and the module's prose count.
