### Module 27 tasks (PLAN-M27) · common rules
- Inputs for every task: `_meta/modules/M27.md` (its section, question or exercise rows, the budget, "Module 15 handoff", "Other handoffs", "Lab decision", "Facts established while planning" F1-F29, "Could not be read" C1-C11, the owner map), STYLE-GUIDE §3-§8, QUALITY-BAR B-E, SYLLABUS §4 (27), the module skeleton, the pilot `modules/17-signals.md` and the finished `modules/26-signal-forms.md` and `modules/25-forms-reactive-and-template-driven.md` for Part D depth (read a section, answer or exercise of the same kind before writing; never copy), and `_meta/reviews/REVIEW-M26.md` and `REVIEW-M25.md` § Findings and § Proposed FIX tasks. Re-verify every fact the task uses before writing (lab > package source > official docs/changelog) and record the basis next to the claim; every item under "Could not be read" for the task's section is read or probed first, or the claim is dropped. The facts of M27.md were probed 2026-10-11 on Angular 22.2.1 (`@angular/common` 22.2.1, RxJS 7.8.2, Node 24.21.0) under `ng test` in a scratch copy of `labs/angular`; a task re-runs the ones it quotes under `ng test`. Where M27.md says "probe first", the run wins over the plan: write what the run shows and state the difference in the LOG line.
- **What runs and what is documented** (M27.md "Lab decision"): **every runtime claim is asserted by specs in `labs/angular` under `ng test`** (jsdom; zoneless `TestBed`); **`provideHttpClient(...)` always before `provideHttpClientTesting()`** (F7); fetch-backend claims with `vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fake)` and `vi.unstubAllGlobals()` in `afterEach` (never `FetchFactory`, internal); retry and `timeout` claims under fake timers restored in `finally`, with jitter made deterministic by an injected random source; `httpResource` inside `TestBed.runInInjectionContext`, `TestBed.tick()` to issue a request and **`await TestBed.inject(ApplicationRef).whenStable()` after every `flush`** (F26); XSRF with cookies expired in `afterEach` and same-origin URLs built from `PlatformLocation.href` (`http://_empty_/` in `TestBed`, F23); network errors with `error(new ProgressEvent('error'))` (the `ErrorEvent` overload is deprecated). **No test touches the network.** No task touches `labs/ts-js`, any `package.json`, `angular.json` or a lockfile; **no `zod` import** (C8). Source reads name the file and function (`grep` first, in `labs/angular/node_modules/@angular/common/types/{http,_module-chunk,http-testing}.d.ts` and `fesm2022/{_module-chunk,http,http-testing}.mjs`; `@angular/core/schematics/migrations.json` and `schematics/bundles/http-xhr-backend.cjs`). **Documented, never worded as observed:** the network, CORS and cookies in real browsers and the browser HTTP cache (09's), XHR upload progress events (source read), the SSR transfer cache, `HTTP_FETCH_MAX_RESPONSE_SIZE`, server-side XHR and `NG02801` (32's, C4), Angular docs sentences (from `main`, C1). Scratch only under `mktemp -d` outside the repo, deleted at the end. Specs assert values, statuses, messages, header values, request counts and logs, never wall-clock durations (retry delays are the delay function's return values, F17); no `console.log`; Angular errors by their `NGxxxxx` code. Every fixture component is standalone with a unique `lab-m27-` selector and a `LabM27…` class name. A non-spec file (exercise sources, `legacy/`) is compiled by `ng build`: no `vi`, no `expect`.
- **Corrections to apply from the plan (they override anything older):** status markers only in the STYLE-GUIDE §3.2 forms at the **first body mention in the module**, plain, with the versions of M27.md "Sections" (`[Changed in v22.0: FetchBackend is the default HttpBackend]`, `withFetch` `[Deprecated since v22.0 — remove it, fetch is the default]`, `withXhr` `[Added in v22.0]`, root-provided `HttpClient` `[Changed in v21.0: provided in root, no provideHttpClient needed]`, `HttpClientModule` `[Deprecated since v18.0 — use provideHttpClient(withInterceptorsFromDi())]`, JSONP `[Deprecated since v22.1 — use standard requests with CORS]`, `timeout` `[Added in v20.1]`, `statusText` `[Deprecated since v21.0 — read status]`, `reportProgress` `[Deprecated since v22.0 — use reportUploadProgress and reportDownloadProgress]`, `reportUploadProgress`/`reportDownloadProgress` `[Added in v22.0]`, `httpResource` `[Stable since v22.0]`); no marker on other APIs. **Module 15 handoff:** link Q15.09 and do not re-ask "`HttpClientModule` versus `provideHttpClient`"; state that the literal replacement switches XHR to fetch (F7, F8). **Name the 09/17/23 split:** the platform (`fetch`, XHR, CORS, cookies, the HTTP cache) is 09's, `resource` and the three-way resource choice are 17's, the `retry` operator, `switchMap` cancellation and `shareReplay` are 23's; 27 owns their use in `HttpClient`. Every internal name of C7 is **marked internal at its first use in each section**. No rationale, roadmap or "why the team chose" claim; the `withXhr` typings note about Angular 23 is quoted as the typings' statement, never as a forecast.
- **Angular code** follows STYLE-GUIDE §7.1 (standalone, `inject()`, signals, signal inputs/outputs, built-in control flow with `track`, v22 file names, `protected`/`readonly`, no `any`, no `as` outside a test of a type error, functional interceptors) and RUN.md §10 (at most 4 injected dependencies per class). **Legacy code** lives only under `legacy/` or a `#### Legacy` heading (§2, §4, §9, §11), says what it is, and compiles under `ng build` (or is a `// Partial:` block). Fixtures use the `lab-m27-` prefix and `LabM27…` class names.
- **First-time-pass rules (from REVIEW-M14 to M26; named so they do not recur):**
  - Factual slips: "which backend", "which status", "which message", "which header value", "how many requests", "which order" and "which resource status" statements are checked against the recorded runs (F7-F28) before writing; every number in prose is traced to a test, run or read. A 2xx parse failure is an `HttpErrorResponse` with status 200 (F16); status 0 covers network, timeout and the `NG02824` refusal (F16, F18, F25); the testing backend does not simulate `timeout` (F18); a child `provideHttpClient` is a separate client (F9); a params change resets `httpResource`'s value (F26).
  - Wording of observation: every "in the lab, X" names a test (`Section N: …`, `Q27.NN …`, `E27.N …`) asserting exactly X; no unrecorded run; no unmeasured comparison; a measured comparison names the count.
  - Quotes: a quoted sentence is verbatim from the named file, branch and date; a shortened runtime message carries "(abridged)"; an error code is the one the run gives (`NG02824`, `NG0203`), attributed to the run or the source; a default is attached to the symbol that declares it (`XSRF-TOKEN`/`X-XSRF-TOKEN` are the XSRF defaults, F24).
  - Header and history: the header lists only what the module teaches and cites; history sentences are dated from the CHANGELOG, typings tags or the `npm pack` read of F4 only; CHANGELOG lines above 22.2.1 are never cited as shipped (C1).
  - Markers: at the **first body mention in the module**, not in a later section or in the question bank; a glossary entry never dates what the sources do not date.
  - Owner credit: name the owning module correctly in every link sentence (`AbortController` 04's, Zod 07's, the platform, CORS and cookies 09's, module packaging 15's, multi providers and environment injectors 16's, `resource` 17's, `PendingTasks` 21's, cold Observables 22's, operators, cancellation, retry and `shareReplay` 23's, resolvers 24's, `validateHttp` 26's, state 28's, testing strategy 29's, SSR 32's, security depth 33's, API contracts 41's, authentication 42's).
  - Mermaid: every section has a diagram of the type named in M27.md followed by a line starting `What to notice`; quote every label with brackets, colons, `@`, `*`, `[`, `/`, `?`, `#`, parentheses or `=`; parse each diagram with the mermaid parser before finishing.
  - Misconceptions: **every** bullet gives the correction **and a cause clause**; "Once true: … until …" only from a source read in the task.
  - Terms: define at first use with an owner link (M27.md lists the terms per section); run a first-use script over the module before finishing.
  - Code: each `### Code` subsection has numbered steps (QUALITY-BAR B5); a snippet shows what its caption claims and every helper and the full template it uses; sections 2, 4, 9 and 11 have a `#### Legacy` subsection as M27.md says.
  - Mechanism: "How it actually works" answers at least one "why does it behave like that" from a source read, marked "read from the `@angular/common` 22.2.1 source" with file and function named (each name found by `grep` in the named file), and "read, not run" where no test asserts it.
  - Titles and criteria: a question title counts what the answer gives; Output only where a spec asserts the answer exactly; Output short answers give the result **and the reason**; an exercise criterion's text equals its test title; an alternative offered in an exercise passes the same tests or is removed.
  - Links: questions linked from their section with their own `[Q27.NN](#q27-NN)`; every link to another module's question is checked against that question's heading before writing; links to other modules carry a section or question anchor (28, 29, 32, 33, 37, 41, 42, 43 are planned files: file-level); anchors follow `check-links.mjs` `githubSlug`; `<!-- relink: … -->` only for a target not in the file yet; callouts begin `> [!NOTE]`/`> [!TIP]`/`> [!WARNING]`; the *Framework vs platform* callout is required in §1; a *Coming from the backend* callout is optional (C2) and, if written, reads the Spring Framework 7 reference first and states **Where the analogy breaks**.
  - Narrative voice: no first person; "the lab test … shows".
  - Format: a blank line before every `###` heading and before a list that follows a bold label; no doubled commas; no "SYLLABUS" in reader text; exercise Constraints list what is not covered and only asserted behaviors are criteria.
  - SOURCES: every URL and package file read gets a line in `SOURCES.md`, including code-source lines for each exercise and the outputs spec; a script lists URLs in the module not in SOURCES (must print nothing).
  - Repeats: tell each mechanism once and link it afterwards; the backend matrix (§2), the chain order (§4), the error-shape table (§5), abort and `timeout` (§6), the refresh queue (§7), the XSRF gates (§8) and the `httpResource` lifecycle (§10) are the likeliest to be retold in questions and exercises, and those link the section.
- **Marker regions:** a section's region is its marker line plus the six empty `###` headings under it. A question chunk's region is its marker line plus the question anchors and headings up to the next marker (QB-M27-01 also writes the bank intro at its placeholder). An exercise's region is its marker line, its `<a id>` and its heading (EX-M27-01 also writes the exercise intro; SEC-M27-01 writes the verification paragraph at its placeholder). CLOSE-M27's marker is in `## Summary`; the placeholders for Short on time, Check your understanding and Connections name CLOSE-M27, and the header's Labs line carries a relink comment for CLOSE-M27.
- **Prose budget** (deltas over the module count measured just before the task; M27.md "Budget"): skeleton 1,973 measured 2026-10-11 by `node labs/tools/module-stats.mjs` and the QUALITY-BAR python one-liner (equal). **Per-chunk caps, summing to the CLOSE limit:** SEC-M27-01..11 = 900 / 800 / 800 / 850 / 850 / 850 / 800 / 700 / 700 / 850 / 750 (8,850); EX-M27-01 = 560 (intro included), EX-M27-02..06 = 520 each (3,160); QB-M27-01..06 = 1,130 each, QB-M27-07 = 904 (7,684; QB-M27-01 includes the bank intro of at most 40 words); CLOSE-M27 = 800. 1,973 + 8,850 + 3,160 + 7,684 + 800 = 22,467. **CLOSE at most 22,470 words; REVIEW ceiling 23,370.** Each task measures before and after with both counters, puts both counts in its LOG line, and cuts before finishing if more than 5% over its delta (repeats first; never a diagram, a trap's cause or a "because"). Answer parts average at most 226 words per QB chunk, none above 290.
- Do not repeat what another module owns (M27.md "Owned elsewhere"): `AbortController` and cancellable promises 04; runtime validation with Zod 07; `fetch`, XHR, HTTP semantics, HTTP caching, CORS and cookies 09; module versus provider packaging 15; multi providers and environment injectors 16; `resource` and the resource choice 17; `PendingTasks` 21; cold Observables 22; flattening cancellation, `catchError`, `retry`, `shareReplay`, unsubscription, `rxResource` and cache-then-network 23; resolvers 24; `validateHttp` and server errors on submit 26; server cache as state 28; testing strategy 29; SSR and the transfer cache 32; CSRF depth and token storage 33; API error contracts 41; OAuth/OIDC and JWT 42.
- Module file: replace only the task's own `<!-- TASK: <ID> -->` region, at the end of the task, in a single edit. `<!-- PLACEHOLDER: … -->` lines are replaced by the task named in them. Never touch another task's marker. Labs: only the files M27.md "Lab files" assigns to the task, under `labs/angular/src/app/modules/27-http-client/` (nothing under `labs/ts-js`; other modules' lab files read-only). No test needs the internet. Throwaway probes live only in a scratch directory and are deleted before the task ends.
- Every task adds its sources under `## Module 27 · HttpClient` in `SOURCES.md` (after the last module section present; package reads as `<pkg>@<version>, <file>, read <date>`) and its inline-defined terms under `## Module 27` in `_meta/GLOSSARY-TERMS.md`.
- Acceptance for every task: the module's lab tests pass (`labs/angular`: `npx ng test --watch=false --include='src/app/modules/27-http-client/**/*.spec.ts'` and `npx tsc -p tsconfig.spec.json --noEmit`, plus `npx ng build` when a non-spec file changed, which also compiles `legacy/`); module 26's lab tests still pass; `node labs/tools/check-snippets.mjs . 27` and `node labs/tools/check-links.mjs --planned` exit 0; every URL in the module is in SOURCES.md; only the declared files are new or changed (`git status --short`); the task's marker is gone; no scratch or probe file is left behind; the LOG line gives the part's and the module's prose count.
